### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**项目核心**: SICA (Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation) 是一个 analysis paper，不是 method paper。D060 确认 method improvement 全部失败，D063 将 framing 升级为 "Regime-Dependent Mechanism Analysis with Entropy Decomposition"，目标 EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25，冲 4.0 分。

**核心决策链**:
- D059: Scenario 2 确认，投 May 25 不变
- D060: Method improvement 三路失败 → 转纯 Analysis paper
- D063: Framing 升级 — entropy decomposition + regime mechanism
- D064: 全文数值统一为修正后 tiebreaking SC
- D065: Entropy 不支持 universal selective SICA
- D066: McNemar 0/3 seeds 显著 → claims downgraded to directional trend
- D067: Quality Ladder L1 NULL → bottleneck 在 traces 不在 extractor
- D068: 4-seed replication 进一步弱化 Goldilocks claim

**易错点**:
1. **不要把 SICA 当成有效方法 claim** — 论文核心是 "analysis of why symbolic verification fails under trace dependence"，不是 "SICA works"
2. **Goldilocks zone 只是 directional trend** — 0/3 seeds 统计显著 (D066)，4 seeds 中 1 个 null (D068)，措辞必须谨慎
3. **exp-063 有两个 registry 条目** — exp-063-llama8b-folio204 (PREPARING, 残留) 和 exp-063-llama8b-folio204-16639 (DONE, 真实结果)，不要混淆
4. **3 个 GPU 空闲 (25068 cuda:1/2, 16639 cuda:1)** — deadline 6 天内每张空闲 GPU 都是浪费，接手后应立即规划部署
5. **Figure 2/3 还没更新** — 等最终数据（所有 RUNNING 实验完成后）再 regenerate，这是 submit 前必做项
6. **Paper 写作合规**: 所有数字必须追溯到 registry，禁止 cherry-picking，强度词必须有统计检验支撑，SICA 效果只能说 "directional trend in Goldilocks zone"

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

\"sc_accuracy\": 0.75, \"accuracy_impact\": 0}, \"notes\": \"Cross-trace UNSAT 验证了 Director 假设：cross-answer UNSAT rate (8.7%) 约为 within-trace (0.9%) 的 10x。但主要瓶颈是 Z3 变量命名不一致：56.4% 的 answer pairs 共享零变量，Z3 无法跨 trace 比较。False-True 对最具辨别力 (non-empty 中 46.7% UNSAT)。最终对准确率无影响（仅覆盖 9/204 问题），第 6 个 negative constructive。核心诊断价值：变量命名不一致是 capability trap 的关键机制之一。\"}, \"exp-042-premise-formalization\": I notice test-5q failed historically, and exp-048-llama8b-pw600 is still running on cuda:3. There are three idle GPUs available now that exp-059/060, exp-067, and exp-040 have freed up their resources. Looking at the handoff data, there's a discrepancy—the roadmap shows exp-061 as running, but the handoff indicates it's done with negative results (SC=75.98%, delta=+0.49pp), and exp-063-llama8b-folio204-16639 completed successfully though the metrics suggest it was actually a negative result for LLaMA.

With the May 25 deadline about 6 days away, I need to prioritize filling those three idle GPUs. The handoff shows seed replications for Mistral FOLIO (exp-052, exp-053) are pending, and there's worker_gpu_exp-064 currently running. I should focus on deploying experiments that expand model coverage for Table 1 and add seed replications for statistical significance, starting with the available idle GPU slots. Looking at what's running and what's needed for the paper deadline, I should prioritize filling Table 1 with more model-dataset combinations and regenerating the figures. I'm considering deploying LLaMA and Mistral on LogiQA for cross-dataset coverage, along with Qwen variants on both LogiQA and PW datasets to maximize the experimental coverage on idle GPUs.

Before deploying anything, I need to check which workers are still active and what's actually cached on the servers, then sequence my actions: verify worker status, plan the idle GPU deployments, report to the Director on current progress, and make sure I've reviewed the resource-saturation skill as needed.
```

</details>
